aa5efd58521a4374450dfad32349f2380982b704 [ diff ] 
Add block-aligned selections for translated PDFs

Adds TranslatedSelectionMapper.getBlockAlignedSelection, which returns
the Reading Mode selection that selects the whole blocks containing the
selected text, in the same direction as the original selection. This
will be used to snap selections in the translated Reading Mode panel to
block boundaries, so that they match the selection in the main panel.

The selection boundaries are set in the blocks' parents so that they
stay valid while translation rewrites the blocks' contents.

The mapper is not used yet.

[a11y-image-desc] Use API-key auth for AnchovyProvider and fix Guest profile crash

MantaServiceFactory and UrlLanguageHistogramFactory return nullptr for
Off-The-Record Guest profiles, causing CHECK(manta_service) in
AccessibilityLabelsService::BindImageAnnotator and
ImageAnnotatorClient::GetTopLanguages to crash the browser process.
Construct an anonymous API-key AnchovyProvider directly when
MantaService is unavailable, and null-check url_language_histogram.

Also configure MantaService::CreateAnchovyProvider to unconditionally
use Chrome's anonymous API key instead of Gaia OAuth tokens so no user
identifier is sent (including from Incognito profiles), and pass the
active Chrome channel on non-ChromeOS builds.

AX-Relnotes: n/a

1c9f39264d1652ca80ecf86c239633d79e9e0ee4 [ diff ] 
crypto: Record why there is no unexportable key hardware

Crypto.TPMSupport2 records kNone for every machine without an
unexportable key provider, so it can't say why. On Windows that can be
a missing tbs.dll, no TPM, or a TBS error. On Mac it can be a missing
Secure Enclave or a missing keychain-access-groups entitlement.

Add Crypto.KeyHardwareSupport.Win and .Mac, recorded by the same
startup probe, so they share Crypto.TPMSupport2's delay, subsampling
and denominator. On Windows it records the TPM version TBS reports, or
why it reports none. On Mac it records whether there is a Secure
Enclave and whether the executable has the entitlement.
GetUnexportableKeyProviderApple() now uses the same helper, so the
metric and the provider can't disagree.

The probe runs on a background thread, so the Windows hook simply asks
TBS again instead of caching the reason next to GetTpmVersion().

Test: crypto_unittests --gtest_filter=*UnexportableKeyMetric*

[Focus] Add EnterFocusMode(indices) and an Ephemeral Group Removal Lock
📋 Metadata

tree
fc515ae7a4b110a6be2cca50b90e084b2ed9659a
parent
5d80ea37757482e62ca1b35517f921901983855b [ diff ] 
[Focus] Add EnterFocusMode(indices) and an Ephemeral Group Removal Lock

Non-group focus backs itself with an ephemeral tab group that only
exists while the group is focused. Callers previously had to create that
group with AddToNewGroup(indices, /*is_ephemeral=*/true) and then call
EnterFocusMode(group) themselves. Replace this with an atomic
TabStripModel::EnterFocusMode(indices) overload and drop the
is_ephemeral flag from AddToNewGroup(), so ephemeral groups cannot exist
unfocused.

Add TabStripModel::TakeEphemeralGroupRemovalLock(), a scoped lock the UI
can take during the OnTabGroupFocusChanged() notification of a
user-initiated exit to keep the ephemeral group in the model while its
exit animates; releasing the lock dissolves the group. The lock is
refused for exits that are part of a structural mutation (e.g. moving
the group's tabs to another window), which need the group dissolved
synchronously, and detaching or moving a locked group CHECKs.

Tests that create ephemeral groups now use EnterFocusMode(indices) with
tab group focusing enabled.

TAG=agy
CONV=85ee297d-3d8f-4f35-8e46-4f061ec5bd1e

0d6b7f1a02c0903b82b2d24acdb355d8b7f9b11f [ diff ] 
Stop WPT when port setup fails

Port setup failures currently allow the WPT adapter to load the runner,
and a setup exception skips port cleanup. Check the setup status before
loading the runner and register cleanup before starting port setup.

Use the existing ExitStack to clean up partial setup, with normal Python
exception chaining if cleanup also fails. Cover nonzero setup statuses,
setup exceptions, and interrupted setup.

webnn: normalize negative indices for ScatterND/GatherND
📋 Metadata

2730d2fff09d57511e1e52371fe152d12ac4aba6

Tue Nov 03 06:00:22 2026
tree
69c2d1caea657150801c54e1a25511df001c03ce
parent
06d800cee8aebaba6d6c074cc893ad5ba7d9ab2e [ diff ] 
webnn: normalize negative indices for ScatterND/GatherND

GatherND and ScatterND indices are clamped to stay within the valid
range, but the clamped value can still be negative (indexing from the
end of an axis).

OV NPU GatherND/ScatterND kernels don't support that and will produce
unpredictable results. This CL implements an OV-only workaround for
GatherND/ScatterND that after clamping, shift any negative index to its
equivalent non-negative value:
  where(indices < 0, indices + input_shape[axis], indices)
matching the existing TFLite backend workaround.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Do not unload SODA native library when SodaClientImpl is destroyed
📋 Metadata
commit
81fde48ac423355abe6935418c50f667e9d000cb
[ log ]
author
Frank Liberato <liberato@google.com>
Tue Nov 03 05:57:20 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 03 05:57:20 2026
tree
6107474bb130dfd05089cbbfe931eccba0bd22e5
parent
61f65805412a69133b5d0c604a694112828316c3 [ diff ] 
Do not unload SODA native library when SodaClientImpl is destroyed

Previously, each SodaClientImpl instance owned a non-static
base::ScopedNativeLibrary member (lib_), causing the SODA shared library
(SODA.dll / libsoda.so) to be unloaded via FreeLibrary/dlclose whenever
the last active speech recognition session in the utility process ended
and reloaded from disk on subsequent sessions.

On Windows, SODA.dll statically links code whose C++ static initializer
allocates a Windows Thread Local Storage (TLS) slot via TlsAlloc() on
each DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH without freeing it on dynamic
DLL_PROCESS_DETACH. Repeatedly unloading and reloading SODA.dll across
speech recognition sessions leaks TLS indices in the long-lived speech
recognition utility process until all 1,088 per-process TLS slots are
exhausted and TlsAlloc() aborts the process. Reloading the library also
repeatedly re-registers protobuf descriptors on the utility main thread,
contributing to HangWatcher hangs.

Cache loaded base::ScopedNativeLibrary instances by FilePath in a
process-lifetime base::NoDestructor map inside soda_client_impl.cc so
the SODA binary is loaded once per path and retained for the lifetime of
the utility process, while still destroying the per-session SODA async
instance via DeleteExtendedSodaAsync() in SodaClientImpl::Destroy().

2865464529e519253c621791663a17fc1b0e8810 [ diff ] 
Update prefetch WPTs to use Webdriver Bidi event to wait on status

Building off wpt change: https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/pull/55274

Prefetch speculation-rules tests currently use a fixed two-second delay
after inserting speculation rules. This is both slower than necessary
and unreliable: the delay does not guarantee that the prefetch has
reached the state required by the test.

Use the WebDriver BiDi speculation.prefetchStatusUpdated test hook to
wait for the actual prefetch event instead. Finishing up on previous
changes added to respective repos...Add helpers that:

* Subscribe before inserting speculation rules, avoiding missed early events.
* Wait for the requested status, normally `ready` or `failure`.
* Reject immediately if a prefetch unexpectedly reports `failure`.
* Support both standard single-URL rules and tests that insert rules manually.

Keep the existing timeout-based helpers for backwards compatibility and
for tests where no status event is emitted or navigation must begin
while the prefetch is still running.

Update eligible prefetch WPTs to use the event-based helpers instead of
waiting two seconds.

Force-WPT-Export: true

[Actor] Test that external trigger without FCM stops the service

An external trigger intent adds a pending task in ActorKeyedService.
ActorKeyedService removes it after preparing_to_start_task_timeout_ms
(default 2 minutes) if no FCM message arrives.
ActorForegroundServiceManager has no timeout of its own and only reacts
to the pending count.

Add a test that the foreground service stays bound for that whole window
while the pending count is 1, and stops and unbinds once the count drops
to 0.

[NTP Aurora V2] Let the AI Mode MVT tile title wrap to two lines

The AI Mode tile in Most Visited Tiles forces its title onto one line,
so longer translations of "AI Mode" get cut off with an ellipsis.

This CL:
1. Raises only the max line count for the AI Mode tile.
2. Switches the title to the simple line breaker in
   TileView.setTitleMaxLines(). The default high-quality breaker
   minimizes the line count, so under a large font size a word wider
   than the tile gets split mid-word. The simple breaker breaks at the
   space instead, and the end ellipsis trims any word that still
   overflows. This change also affect the "Add new" button.

Screenshots: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1bMRVyyqOd0-cAmyTbL9IhKNU9uSvDdpdCngSzSXIHnk/edit?tab=t.0#bookmark=id.h4n9j4n2bxhp

[base] Rename Feature::WasAccessedEarly() with ForTesting suffix

After crrev.com/c/8669086 removed the early-access CHECK in
FeatureList::EnableRuntimeMutability(), Feature::WasAccessedEarly()
and the internal FeatureWasAccessedEarly() helper have no remaining
production callers and are only called from unit tests in
base/feature_list_unittest.cc.

This change renames Feature::WasAccessedEarly() to
Feature::WasAccessedEarlyForTesting() and inlines the flag check.

[SVG] Don't scale a percentage textPath startOffset by pathLength
📋 Metadata
commit
5ab691d0d4292d7cc45d8d23741ae7c3977eb28e
[ log ]
author
Sarah Drasner <sdrasner@google.com>
Tue Nov 03 05:06:42 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 03 05:06:42 2026
tree
2e79ea9937b60eabfd4750c8a90dcb391aac9b2c
parent
599789097af11286fca074380e8a3634cea2fa25 [ diff ] 
[SVG] Don't scale a percentage textPath startOffset by pathLength

SVG 2 says that pathLength "has no effect on percentage distance-along-
a-path calculations", and that when pathLength is zero only
"non-percentage" offsets greater than zero become +Infinity [1]. In
other words, startOffset="50%" should always land halfway along the
path as the browser measured it, whatever pathLength says.

We were resolving percentage offsets against the author's pathLength
and then multiplying by the pathLength scale factor on top of that.
With a non-zero pathLength the two steps cancel out, which is why this
went unnoticed. With pathLength="0" the percentage resolves to 0 and
the text ends up at the start of the path.

Resolve percentages against the computed path length instead, and
only apply the scale factor to non-percentage offsets.

This also brings the 2018 WPT svg/path/distance/pathLength-zero-
percentage.svg in line with the current spec text: it expected the old
behaviour and now contradicts the newer textpath-pathlength-zero-
offset-percentage.svg. The sibling pathLength-positive-ref.svg already
shows these percentages unaffected by pathLength, so I think we can
point it there and drop its own reference.

[1] https://svgwg.org/svg2-draft/paths.html#PathLengthAttribute

R=fs@opera.com

[Focus] Skip Tab Group Focus IPHs During Session/Window Restore

Suppresses triggering the Unfocus IPH and Focus discovery promo when a
browser window is created with an initial focused tab group from Session
Restore or Tab Restore, while allowing subsequent user-initiated focus
changes in the same window to show the promo normally.

Move BrowserWindowThemeObserver into its own module

Relocates BrowserWindowThemeObserver from the flat chrome/browser/ui
directory into chrome/browser/ui/browser_window_theme_observer/ with a
dedicated BUILD.gn, mirroring sibling window-scoped features such as
browser_active_state_manager/ and zoom/browser_window_zoom_observer.

This collapses the previous header/impl source_set split into a single
target and gives the feature its own unit_tests target, so future
changes (NativeTheme bridging, test utilities) have a clear home and
precisely scoped deps per the BrowserWindowFeatures modularization
guidelines.

Pure move; no functional change.

Bug: None

3667caf92d0f4389077409ab261dcf01e071de59
parent
a3c8689f8aca956922986f4cffe6d6acb4889f8d [ diff ] 
Optimize lens::AreSearchUrlsEquivalent()

Switch lens::AreSearchUrlsEquivalent() to use the faster
net::UrlSearchParamsView rather than net::UrlSearchParams, since it
doesn't need the lifetime of the view to exceed the lifetime of the URL.

Also use the parameter removal functionality built in to
UrlSearchParamsView rather than the slower
net::AppendOrReplaceQueryParameter().

This will enable removal of UrlSearchParams in a follow-up CL.

273ad725c7831fe5e6439feb7956a62532dfb936 [ diff ] 
[AV2] Split shareable behavior from DAV1D

Extract the codec-agnostic behavior from Dav1dVideoDecoder into a new
DavXdVideoDecoderBase base class so Dav1dVideoDecoder (and a follow-up
Dav2dVideoDecoder) can share threading, callback binding,
FrameBufferPool picture allocation, monochrome UV plane synthesis, HDR
metadata reordering, and VideoFrame wrapping.

Plane wrapping in BindImageToVideoFrame now slices subspans directly
from the FrameBufferPool allocation rather than using UNSAFE_BUFFERS;
the only remaining UNSAFE_BUFFERS calls are the pre-existing C-callback
StringPrintV logger and ITU-T T.35 metadata spans moved from
Dav1dVideoDecoder.

Cq-Exclude-Trybots: luci.chromium.try:win_optional_gpu_tests_rel

c755e416b2be0328bbf6a5b33ca253108626bd81
parent
c0451dbeedf0eacb4916c97b402992ca5291e5f7 [ diff ] 
crostini: Fix crash in BaguetteInstaller::OnListVmDisks

When ListVmDisks fails over D-Bus, OnListVmDisks receives std::nullopt,
logs a warning, and previously fell through into dereferencing
`response->success()` and `response->images()`, triggering a libc++
hardening crash. When `response` was present with `success() == false`,
it also still inspected `response->images()`.

Chain the checks in OnListVmDisks with `else if` so that a nullopt or
unsuccessful ListVmDisks response logs the warning and falls back to
fetching the Baguette image URL via GetBaguetteImageUrl.

Also add the missing early return in OnConciergeAvailable when
vm_concierge is unavailable so it does not invoke ListVmDisks with a
moved-from callback, remove the unused GetBaguetteImageUrl declaration
from BaguetteInstaller, and add unit tests covering BaguetteInstaller.

Bug: 492645799
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter="BaguetteInstallerTest.*"

[Viz] Support sub-one backing scales for retained OOPIFs

Retained OOPIF root passes currently clamp their requested backing scale
to at least 1. This works for magnification but not when an OOPIF is
embedded with a scale below 1.

Honor valid backing scales below 1.

137c0ddb4d256805c4ac7979880be80b683bc1dc
parent
2202b48d03865914641bb60034793c4d8ae8d0ab [ diff ] 
Harden BrowserWindowThemeObserver

Tightens the window-scoped theme observation bridge ahead of migrating
consumers from direct ThemeServiceObserver inheritance to its
listener-style RegisterThemeChangedCallback() API:

- CHECK the ThemeService returned by ThemeServiceFactory before
  observing it, matching BrowserWidget::GetColorProviderKey(). Every
  profile a browser window can have gets a ThemeService, so a missing
  one is a programming error rather than a state to tolerate.
- Add a SEQUENCE_CHECKER and assert registration, notification and
  destruction happen on the owning (UI) sequence.
- Route OnThemeChanged() through NotifyThemeChanged() so there is a
  single dispatch path.
- Add a const From() overload, matching other BrowserWindowFeatures
  (e.g. DesktopBrowserWindowCapabilities, BrowserActiveStateManager).
- Expand header documentation describing ownership, RAII subscription
  semantics and threading expectations.

No behavior change for existing callers.

Bug: None
Test: autoninja -C out/Default chrome/browser/ui:browser_window_theme_observer_impl

8aaba1cdd5e72fadaa2bc944d6e0e66809d6071a [ diff ] 
[Actor] Resolve validation surfaces within the task's profile

ExecutionEngine looked up an action's validation surface with
ActorSurfaceHandle::Get(), which is process-wide and doesn't check the
profile. Look it up through the task profile's ActorSurfaceRegistry
instead, so a surface from another profile is treated as absent before
the engine reads anything from it.

Added ExecutionEngineCrossProfileBrowserTest.WaitCannotObserveCrossProfileTab:
a Wait action observing a tab in another profile now fails as if the
tab doesn't exist (kTabWentAway).

7519953bc6c86c80666a94a15ddedc73bb3273ef
parent
bdcf73b0c1fdecd76906ecc60f593319846083f8 [ diff ] 
Bound default JSON whitespace in on-device responseConstraint

llguidance allows unlimited whitespace between JSON tokens by default.
When generating JSON, a model can get stuck emitting spaces and newlines
until the session hits its token limit.

Set a default x-guidance whitespace_pattern on JSON object schemas so
each gap allows at most one newline and bounded spaces, preserving
property key order. Reject non-object schemas and caller-supplied
x-guidance blocks. MakeConstraint also rejects embedded NUL bytes before
passing constraint strings to llguidance.

[Unified tabstrip] Fix tab group header reordering and keypress checks

Pass the header's layout orientation to
GetReorderCommandForKeyboardEvent() instead of hardcoding vertical so
keyboard reordering and announcements work in both orientations, and
rename ShiftGroupUp()/ShiftGroupDown() to
ShiftGroupPrevious()/ShiftGroupNext().

Also guard group header collapse toggling and context menu display
until the editor bubble is closed, and add unit test coverage for
these behaviors.

1b8d75824506a7f124b138cd038c2d7c0037b566
parent
fdd33782cd2bda780e5f969a1474d0324771bb10 [ diff ] 
[CRD Linux] Add host type hooks and detection

Prep work for the custom, x11 and auto-session host types, which need
start-host and migrate-host to set up and tear down per-type state
(the session routing file and session units) and to know which host
type is currently running.

- HostTypeEnvironment wraps systemctl and the session routing file
  path/owner so that host type logic can be unit tested.
- HostType gets requires_local_user(), OnStartHost() and OnStopHost()
  hooks, and session_unit_prefix() for types that keep a per-user
  session unit running.
- HostType::DetectCurrent() detects the running host type from the
  system state: chrome-remote-desktop@USER active => single-process,
  chrome-remote-desktop.service inactive => none, no routing file =>
  gdm-managed, then types whose session unit is enabled or active.
  Until the custom and auto-session types exist, any other running
  multi-process host is reported as gdm-managed.
- BackUpAndRemoveSessionRoutingConfig() renames the routing file to
  session_routing.json.bak, so that hand-written configs are never
  lost.
- gdm-managed's start hook moves a leftover routing file away using
  that helper, rather than deleting it, since the file may be a
  user's hand-written config.

Nothing calls the hooks or DetectCurrent() yet, so start-host and
migrate-host behave as before.

📋 Metadata

7da45af78655485e90144d9024142bcc01740ca7

author

tree
3aaea06072e413fcd70bd45a5d2d86b93b18b80f
parent
70a46fffbeaef415061ae311ac16c3c1e017b963 [ diff ] 
[MSE] Clean up cross-document MediaSource state on context destruction

When a main-thread MediaSource's execution context (such as an iframe)
is destroyed:
- Clear media_source_attachment_ and attachment_tracer_ even if the
  MediaSource was still in kClosed because attachment had started but
  not yet completed, rather than retaining strong/traced references to
  the attachment and surviving media element.
- Reject StartAttachingToMediaElement() when context_already_destroyed_
  is set instead of DCHECKing, since an object URL registered in another
  document remains valid after the MediaSource's document is detached.
- Guard SeekableInternal(), OnTrackChanged(), and OnElementError() in
  SameThreadMediaSourceAttachment when media_source_context_destroyed_
  is set, matching BufferedInternal() and OnElementTimeUpdate().

5ce54bfa3cf6c532f537d99be49c63979992c750 [ diff ] 
[Linux] Don't use logind for session end in a snap

snapd's AppArmor policy for a strictly confined browser snap has no
rules for org.freedesktop.login1, so SessionEndListenerLinux can't work
there: the Inhibit call is denied and logs an AppArmor denial on every
startup, and PrepareForShutdown can't be received either.

Skip the logind proxy when base::nix::GetAppSandbox() is kSnap. The
GNOME SessionManager listener is unchanged, and the Inhibit portal
session monitor still reports the end of the session in a snap.

Tested with a Chromium snap (chrome/installer/linux/snap) on Ubuntu
24.04 with snapd 2.77.1: the login1 Inhibit denial at startup is gone.

7595782f9ccc5b115f7921e25b929cc0b126f7e2

cc7c2b7910205494375092110631451a20d1d5e4 [ diff ] 
[policy] Reload platform policies when management state changes

On Windows, the platform policy loader decides whether to filter
sensitive policies based on the device management state that
PlatformManagementService caches in Local State. The cache is refreshed
asynchronously at startup, but nothing reloads the policies when the
refresh changes the state, e.g. if the device joined Azure AD since the
last time Chrome ran. Sensitive policies then stay blocked until the
next periodic reload, up to 15 minutes later.

Refresh the cache through ChromeBrowserPolicyConnector and reload the
platform policies when the management trustworthiness used for policy
loading changed. Only the platform provider is reloaded, so this doesn't
trigger cloud policy fetches.

This change was written with AI assistance.

5d1cb7b2f1a109b3b0feca6c018a7dffb05f97cf [ diff ] 
[policy] Fetch missing management state for initial policy load

On Windows, sensitive platform policies are only honored on managed
devices. The initial policy load reads management state from Local State
because getting it requires blocking OS calls. When nothing is cached
(e.g. on first run), the device is considered unmanaged and sensitive
policies are blocked.

Add a blocking variant of the trustworthiness getter that synchronously
fetches the management state if not cached, and use it for the initial
policy load. A blocking call is safe here because
AsyncPolicyLoader::InitialLoad() runs synchronously during early startup
before thread pools spin up, where blocking is permitted. The new method
asserts base::AssertBlockingAllowed(). Other status providers are also
updated to support this early call.

DanglingUntriaged-notes: PrefService cache lifetime is managed
externally.

This change was written with AI assistance.

861dd679700f1e34f5d72ce2382c45240ae64ba0

tree
0ddaeb606c5f653f1bad5efe285c00d2a16bbcd5
parent
6bfefa17382aa97c15f71e781aa51a4e381c33a4 [ diff ] 
Disable the updater WebView2 UI in elevated processes

The WebView2 runtime always runs msedgewebview2.exe at medium integrity,
regardless of the host's elevation. An elevated updater therefore can't
give WebView2 a profile that is both writable by the browser process and
safe for the elevated process to access: Program Files isn't writable by
the browser, and a user-writable location would expose the elevated
process to tampering with the profile or planted reparse points. The
WebView2 loader also honors user-controlled overrides, such as
WEBVIEW2_BROWSER_EXECUTABLE_FOLDER, which an elevated process should not
act on.

Fail WebView2ProgressWnd::Initialize() when the process is admin, before
any WebView2 API call, so that elevated installs fall back to the native
UI without starting msedgewebview2.exe. In a non-elevated process, use
the per-user install directory for the profile regardless of the updater
scope on the command line.

d7351903ba17052c63f4b25e017ad9ace956de08 [ diff ] 
[contextual_cueing] Only observe category classifier if history sync is enabled

Gate AnnotationType::kCategoryClassifier observer registration in
ContextualCueingController (when kContextualCueingV2MultiSource is
disabled) and in GlicCueTabState and ContextualSearchCueTabState (when
kContextualCueingV2MultiSource is enabled) on History Sync
(syncer::UserSelectableType::kHistory) being enabled.

Because PageCategoryClassifierBridgeImpl is demand-driven via
SetDemandActive(), gating kCategoryClassifier observer registration in
these downstream consumers prevents the category classifier from running
when History Sync is disabled while keeping
//components/page_content_annotations decoupled from //components/sync.

TAG=agy
CONV=da5e5b26-15d0-48cf-a1f5-5b2977965e4e

when the list ends.

which suggest indented body text was misread as nested items.

[Reading mode PDFs] Record OrderedListLookaheadLines

Record the number of intervening lines scanned to confirm an ordered
list start, or the overflow bucket when the 15-line limit is reached
without confirming.

This shows whether the 15-line limit fits real PDFs. If most lists
confirm within a few lines, the limit can be lowered to reduce false
starts; frequent overflows may mean lists with long items are missed.

parent
0556e76cf7074a8fba7fec410c816ed7a8a72510 [ diff ] 
Use skia.mojom.SkColor for colors in blink.mojom.Manifest

theme_color, background_color, dark_theme_color and
dark_background_color were sent as raw uint32 values. Use the dedicated
skia.mojom.SkColor type instead so the IPC describes what the values
are.

skia.mojom.SkColor is typemapped to ::SkColor for both the regular and
Blink C++ bindings. Make blink::Manifest store these colors as SkColor
too, so it keeps mirroring the mojom struct, drop the now-redundant
static_cast<SkColor>() in ManifestToWebAppInstallInfoJob and the DevTools
page handler, and have ManifestParser return SkColor instead of RGBA32.
Nothing in Java or WebUI reads these fields.

NO_IFTTT=Type-only change; manifest.cc copy/compare blocks need no update

parent
3783bcb19e9fb8d2d8c1e13e0f1903439a7539bd [ diff ] 
[Views] Optimize child Widget theme updates and BrowserWidget key

When features::kThemeChangeOptimization is enabled:

- Avoid observing NativeTheme directly on child Widgets without an
  explicit NativeTheme override, as they already receive updates via
  ParentThemeObserver.

- Allow child Widgets to short-circuit unchanged ColorProviderKeys in
  ParentThemeObserver::OnColorProviderChanged() instead of calling
  ResetLastColorProviderKey() unconditionally, while resetting owned
  child keys in BrowserWidget::UserChangedTheme() when custom
  ThemeProvider assets may change.

- Transition child Widgets back to observing NativeTheme on parent
  ColorProviderSource destruction without scheduling a spurious
  ThemeChanged(), and guard scheduled and synchronous theme updates
  against running during or after NativeWidget destruction.

Also avoid redundant GetCustomTheme() and AppBrowserController lookups
in BrowserWidget::GetColorProviderKey().

parent
f10954fa8800b99813f4ad2b0873cb942aa2df52 [ diff ] 
WebauthnBrowserBridge: Adopt JniUniquePtr

WebauthnBrowserBridge stores its native
::webauthn::WebauthnBrowserBridge pointer in raw long field
mNativeWebauthnBrowserBridge. Passing raw integer handles across the JNI
boundary bypasses type checking and smart pointer lifecycle enforcement,
risking use-after-free bugs if the native object is accessed after
destruction.

Migrate mNativeWebauthnBrowserBridge to JniUniquePtr with a
package-private JniTypeToken mapped to ::webauthn::WebauthnBrowserBridge
via @JniType. Return jni_zero::MakeUnique from the C++ factory, destroy
the owned instance via JniUniquePtr.destroy(), and pass JniPtr to
@NativeMethods calls.

XrActivityListener: Adopt JniRawPtr

XrActivityListener stores its native ::webxr::XrActivityListener pointer
in raw long field mNativeXrActivityListener. Passing raw integer handles
across the JNI boundary bypasses compile-time type checking and
smart-pointer lifetime tracking.

Migrate mNativeXrActivityListener to JniRawPtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::webxr::XrActivityListener via @JniType.
Construct the borrowed reference via jni_zero::MakeRaw, release it via
JniRawPtr.release() on teardown, and pass JniPtr to @NativeMethods
calls.

OverlayPanel: Use JniUniquePtr in panel content

Passing the native OverlayPanelContent pointer across JNI as a raw long
bypasses type safety and obscures that Java owns the native instance
lifecycle until destroy() is called.

Migrate OverlayPanelContent to own its native peer via
JniUniquePtr<NativeOverlayPanelContent> and pass short-lived references
via JniPtr<NativeOverlayPanelContent>.

safety and leaves C++ lifetime management implicit.

and released by Java.

7ec4aa31f7a01ba5046f18cdff836ad0cf71863d

616ddb1e6007e769a515160b4164293d2d6b859e [ diff ] 
ArCoreInstallUtils: Adopt JniRawPtr

ArCoreInstallUtils stores its native ::webxr::ArCoreInstallHelper
pointer in raw long field mNativeArCoreInstallUtils. Passing raw integer
handles across the JNI boundary bypasses type checking and lifetime
validation, risking use-after-free bugs when the C++ counterpart is
destroyed.

Migrate mNativeArCoreInstallUtils to JniRawPtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::webxr::ArCoreInstallHelper via @JniType.
Construct the borrowed reference via jni_zero::MakeRaw, release it via
JniRawPtr.release() on teardown, and pass JniPtr to @NativeMethods
calls.

55220575f3f0732f7b0eebc5943e40a83f6a5672

f34bff24b991a71af95daa152750f737a01be963 [ diff ] 
UI: Migrate ResourceManager to JniRawPtr

ResourceManager stores its native ::ui::ResourceManagerImpl pointer in a
raw Java long field. Passing untyped integer handles across the JNI
boundary bypasses compile-time type checking and lifetime tracking,
risking use-after-free bugs when the native object is destroyed.

Declare a package-private NativeResourceManagerImpl JniTypeToken mapped
to ::ui::ResourceManagerImpl and store the borrowed reference in a
JniRawPtr<NativeResourceManagerImpl> created via jni_zero::MakeRaw. Pass
the reference to @NativeMethods calls via
JniPtr<NativeResourceManagerImpl> and release the handle on native
teardown.

tree
5c83439e91e326401a2dd0cfe4db01ef1cda258f
parent
d51500efda74ebd8adec44a0edc285d63c04b607 [ diff ] 
[CRD Linux] Terminate CRD-created sessions when the host stops

When session_routing.json sets createRemoteUserSessions, the
multi-process host asks GDM to create a graphical session for the user
if none exists. These sessions currently outlive the host service, so
after migrating to another host mode (or uninstalling the host) a CRD-
created GNOME session keeps running for the user, which conflicts with
the next mode since most desktops support only one session per user.

Record the sessions that UserDesktopSessionBackend created in
CreatedSessionTracker, and terminate them in TerminateAllSessions(),
which only runs when the daemon receives SIGTERM (e.g. systemctl stop,
migrate-host, package removal). Before terminating a session, check
that it still exists and still matches the recorded session ID, UID,
username, and Remote=yes, since logind session IDs can be reused.
Sessions that CRD didn't create are never terminated. Sessions are
terminated in parallel, and entries whose termination fails are kept so
that the next stop retries them.

Host upgrades and config updates kill the daemon without running
cleanup, so CRD-created sessions are still kept and re-adopted in those
cases.

ef6f7f0b76d99bf3a43f8559e866f4621a5c1232 [ diff ] 
OfflinePages: Remove unused native peer in DownloadBridge

OfflinePageDownloadBridge stored a raw long
mNativeOfflinePageDownloadBridge returned by
JNI_OfflinePageDownloadBridge_Init, which heap-allocated a C++
OfflinePageDownloadBridge instance that had no instance methods and was
never passed to any operational JNI call or destroyed by the Java
singleton.

Remove the unused C++ instance state and Java native pointer field so
OfflinePageDownloadBridgeJni.init() registers the profile-creation
callback without leaking a heap allocation.

tree
fd45411e03ca4289c1fcba615d33ae02cb3b5a96
parent
a30c499836f6f49be8318ef82bbe046ed8632242 [ diff ] 
XrSessionCoordinator: Adopt JniRawPtr

XrSessionCoordinator stores its native ::webxr::XrSessionCoordinator
pointer in raw long field mNativeXrSessionCoordinator. Passing raw
integer handles across the JNI boundary bypasses type checking and
lifetime validation, risking use-after-free bugs when the C++
counterpart is destroyed.

Migrate mNativeXrSessionCoordinator to JniRawPtr with a package-private
JniTypeToken mapped to ::webxr::XrSessionCoordinator via @JniType.
Construct the borrowed reference via jni_zero::MakeRaw, release it via
JniRawPtr.release() on teardown, and pass JniPtr to @NativeMethods
calls.

Migrate AdsBlockedDialog to JniRawPtr

Passing raw long pointers across JNI bypasses PartitionAlloc/BackupRefPtr
protections and risks Use-after-Free when the native AdsBlockedDialog is
destroyed.

Wrap the native pointer in JniRawPtr<NativeAdsBlockedDialog> created via
jni_zero::MakeRaw(this) and release the borrowed reference in
dismiss() and onDismiss() before the native instance is destroyed.

[browser_actuator] Add Keychain store for the colocation proof (Mac)

Chrome keeps each profile's co-location proof in the Data Protection
Keychain so another Google app on the same Mac can read it, including
while Chrome is closed.

WriteColocationProofToKeychain() upserts a generic password in the
<team ID>.<bundle ID>.local-actuation access group, keyed by the
profile's sync client ID, not synchronizable, and accessible after
first unlock on this device only. DeleteColocationProofFromKeychain()
treats a missing item as success. Calls go through
crypto::apple::KeychainV2 so the tests run on ScopedFakeKeychainV2
(extended in the parent CL). The attribute set has the same shape as
icloud_recovery_key_mac.mm, minus iCloud sync and with this-device-only
accessibility; the access group string is built from chrome_version.h
like unexportable_key_provider_config.cc.

The entitlement for the access group lands separately. Nothing calls
these functions yet; the service follows.

84fa15d8033a497a7c38811f749a70629d7063e5
parent
da9d4e061a6cc805cfed85a70056f39ec47faeb6 [ diff ] 
[Mac] Avoid duplicate child window reveal locks in immersive fullscreen

Relaunching Chrome (e.g. after an update) when a browser window was in
a macOS Split View fullscreen space crashes the browser on startup if a
child window (such as a permission prompt, bubble, or find bar) is open.

AppKit sends -[NSWindowDelegate windowDidEnterFullScreen:] twice when
restoring into Split View: once when entering fullscreen and again when
the Dock finishes tiling the window into the space. As a result,
FullscreenTransitionCompleted() runs twice and tries to acquire reveal
locks for existing overlay child windows a second time, hitting
CHECK(!window_lock_received_.contains(child)).

Only establish initial child window reveal locks and start child window
observation on the first FullscreenTransitionCompleted() notification.

Tested: views_unittests --gtest_filter=*FullscreenTransitionCompleted*

[Vertical Tabs] Migrate NestedTabReorderUtils drag reordering to Token

Prior to this CL, NestedTabReorderUtils relied on
TabModel.getRelatedTabList() and a helper getRepresentativeTabId() to
perform group reordering, index calculation, and solitary child tab
checks. This required inspecting tab IDs to infer group boundaries and
incurred unnecessary list allocations instead of directly using the
group ID token.

This CL updates NestedTabReorderUtils and its callers to use Token-based
lookups (TabModel.getTabsInGroup() and TabModel.getTabCountForGroup()),
passes currentGroupId into moveTabOrGroup(), and replaces the
representative tab ID helper with scoped tab ID resolution for group
headers.

[win] Record 30s extrema and low-Available intervals in SystemMemoryLists

SystemMemoryListMetricsProvider samples
NtQuerySystemInformation(SystemMemoryListInformation) every 100ms [1]
and every 30s records counts of samples with lists <= 2 MiB and the
end-of-window values of Free/Zero/Modified/StandbyPageCount [2].

Our working hypothesis for Windows memory management is [3]:
1. Working Set -> Modified List: Normal-priority working sets are
   trimmed when Available drops below ~250-350 MiB or falls rapidly
   (>= 100 MiB/s), while low-memory-priority (Priority-2) working sets
   are trimmed at any Available level.
2. Modified List -> Compression Store: When the Modified list reaches
   ~7-8% of RAM, it drains into the compression store within 1-3s.
3. Compression Store -> Pagefile: When Available drops below ~10-11% of
   RAM, the compression store is flushed to the pagefile, moving pages
   to the Standby list and bringing Available back to ~15% of RAM.

Current metrics cannot fully validate this hypothesis:
- LowIntervalsPerThirtySeconds.AllLists only counts samples <= 2 MiB,
  which is almost never reached (0 in >99% of Windows Stable UMA
  records [3]).
- 30s page-list snapshots and 2-minute AvailableMemoryMB snapshots are
  too coarse to catch transient sub-second Available troughs (~250-350
  MiB), Modified-list peaks (~7-8% of RAM), or within-30s Available
  rebounds to ~15% of RAM [3].

This CL records additional Memory.SystemMemoryLists.* metrics from the
existing 100ms samples every 30s:
- MinAvailableMemory{MB,Percent}: 30s minimum Available memory.
- MaxAvailableMemoryAfterMin{MB,Percent}: maximum Available memory
  observed at or after the 30s minimum.
- MaxModifiedPageCount: 30s peak Modified page list size.
- IntervalsBelow{150MB,250MB,350MB,SwapLine10Pct}: 100ms samples per
  30s with Available memory below each threshold.

We will use these metrics to:
1. Working Set -> Modified List: Confirm that there is a strong
   correlation between UMA records with MinAvailableMemoryMB < 250-350
   MiB (or high counts in IntervalsBelow{150MB,250MB,350MB}) and a low
   ResidentSet / PMF ratio.
2. Modified List -> Compression Store: Use MaxModifiedPageCount to check
   whether the 30s Modified-list peak caps around ~7-8% of RAM.
3. Compression Store -> Pagefile: Compare MinAvailableMemoryPercent vs.
   MaxAvailableMemoryAfterMinPercent to check whether 30s windows that
   dip below ~10-11% Available rebound to ~15% Available (this is not
   perfect because a rebound may complete after the 30s window closes,
   but it is a good approximation since flushes typically complete
   within a few seconds).
4. Correlate these memory metrics with speed/congestion metrics to
   determine the magnitude of slowdowns when working sets are trimmed,
   the Modified list is drained to the compression store, and the
   compression store is flushed to the pagefile.

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:chrome/browser/metrics/system_memory_list_metrics_provider_win.cc;l=127-149;drc=dc4601072eecf55ca3f1ee0b211fc4486b20afed
[2] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:chrome/browser/metrics/system_memory_list_metrics_provider_win.cc;l=108-272;drc=dc4601072eecf55ca3f1ee0b211fc4486b20afed
[3] http://artifacts/OSoLsDcJsR

[MiraclePtr] Add RAW_PTR_EXCLUSION to HWINEVENTHOOK containers

`HWINEVENTHOOK` is an opaque Win32 handle (`DECLARE_HANDLE`) that is
only typedef'd as a pointer for type checking and is never allocated by
PartitionAlloc.

This advances MiraclePtr's expansion to containers and preempts an
upcoming clang-plugin update that will strictly ban these un-annotated
instances.

Doc: go/miracleptr-in-containers

adade92ba04a4037bafe6cbf47da753d1799b1c8

01bbb9ca23e22532d8d082a04c7c3750520abf1b
parent
b282dc5152e2c64aeb76bd8cb97f5f42a41d014f [ diff ] 
[MSE] Don't open a MediaSource whose context died mid-attachment

If the context owning a MediaSource (e.g. a same-origin iframe) is
destroyed after a media element in another document starts attaching
to it, but before the element's player opens its ChunkDemuxer, then
SameThreadMediaSourceAttachment::CompleteAttachingToMediaElement()
would still open the already-closed MediaSource and hand it a
WebMediaSource referencing the player's demuxer.

Since SameThreadMediaSourceAttachment::Close() skips closing a
MediaSource whose context is destroyed, nothing released that
WebMediaSource before the player destroyed the demuxer, so subsequent
MediaSource calls (e.g. reading duration) used the freed demuxer.

Like CrossThreadMediaSourceAttachment already does, skip completing
the attachment if the MediaSource's context has been destroyed.

Fixed: 577171064

96f2cbaec0aceb776741f803d07fb8d8eec2c446
parent
dbca8a38d5542a19c8ae834857f6abb53cc0ba5d [ diff ] 
History: Make HistoryDeletionInfo pure Java value

Passing C++ pointers as raw long integers across the JNI boundary
bypasses type safety and MiraclePtr protections.

HistoryDeletionInfo previously stored a raw pointer to a C++
history::DeletionInfo and queried its fields through three JNI
callbacks. The C++ DeletionInfo was stack-allocated and only valid
for the duration of the synchronous notification.

Instead of passing a transient native pointer across JNI, extract the
deleted URLs and time range validity flags in C++ and pass them
directly to HistoryDeletionInfo.create(), eliminating all JNI callbacks
and the native pointer field.

Bug: 40794873

SurveyUiDelegateBridge. SurveyUiDelegateAndroid borrows the pointer
to Java and clears it upon C++ destruction via clearNativePtr.
Remove unused native pointer parameter from createFromMessage.

Own ScopedBatchAndroid via JniUniquePtr

829123ab01adab165a0c6aeba52089bf560e87eb
[ log ]
author

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 03 01:24:08 2026
tree
dcdeed4e849502fb0b486d04e6ad59789aacba0a
parent
dff096ff637a2afbafbf3e15201a808e871fbd13 [ diff ] 
Own ScopedBatchAndroid via JniUniquePtr

ScopedStorageBatchImpl holds a raw long mNativeScopedBatch and calls
commitBatch(long) on close(), where C++ reinterpret_casts the long and
deletes ScopedBatchAndroid. Passing a raw long across JNI bypasses type
safety and LifetimeAssert checks.

Return JniUniquePtr<NativeScopedBatchAndroid> from createBatch() via
jni_zero::MakeUnique and call mNativeScopedBatch.destroy() directly in
ScopedStorageBatchImpl.close(), removing the redundant commitBatch JNI
method.

5d6d56e4a2215a9e55af629b946fb65297f3fcb7
parent
7d4999605a6244adf13c9d9a31519c2590dde8ee [ diff ] 
WebUIToolbar: Fix ToolbarView browsertests with WebUIToolbar

When `InitialWebUI` and `WebUIToolbar` are enabled, several tests in
`chrome/browser/ui/views/toolbar/toolbar_view_browsertest.cc` fail or
crash because they assume native Views layout margins or `FlexLayout`
overflow state:

1. `ToolbarViewUnitTest.IsPositionInWindowCaption_EmptyArea`:
   When `IsWebUIAppMenuButtonEnabled()` is true, `ToolbarView` zeroes
   its trailing `FlexLayout` interior margin so `toolbar_webview_`
   spans to the trailing edge and applies the margin in CSS. Check that
   the right-edge midpoint is treated as caption only when
   `!features::IsWebUIAppMenuButtonEnabled()` and not caption otherwise,
   while keeping the top and bottom interior margin checks
   unconditional.

2. `ToolbarViewResponsiveTest.OmniboxTwoStageFlexResizingAndButtonLifecycle`:
   When `IsWebUIToolbarFullyEnabled()` is true, `ToolbarView` does not
   create `ToolbarController` because responsive layout and overflow are
   handled in WebUI JavaScript (covered by
   `WebUIToolbarFullyEnabledBrowserTest`). Skip this C++ `FlexLayout`
   and `ToolbarController` test when `IsWebUIToolbarFullyEnabled()` is
   true.

3. `ToolbarViewContextualTasksInteriorMarginBrowserTest`,
   `ToolbarViewDefaultInteriorMarginBrowserTest`, and
   `ToolbarViewCircularContextualTasksBrowserTest`:
   These Views-specific interior margin tests previously disabled only
   `kWebUIBackForwardButton`. Also disable `kWebUIAppMenuButton` and
   the umbrella `kWebUIToolbar` feature, since enabling either button
   in WebUI zeroes `ToolbarView`'s leading or trailing `FlexLayout`
   interior margin.

Shrink CSSCustomIdentValue by 8 bytes on 64-bit

The 4-byte CSSPropertyID was declared after the AtomicString, leaving a
4-byte hole before the AtomicString and 4 bytes of tail padding.
Declare it before the AtomicString to fill the hole.

sizeof(CSSCustomIdentValue):
  64-bit (all platforms): 32 -> 24 bytes
  32-bit:                 unchanged (20 bytes)

These values hold custom identifiers and property names in parsed style
sheets, e.g. transition-property, will-change and animation-name.

No behavior change.

tree
01d4906a53052435731ffe404c7ea646e327979a
parent
e8573e4dffa0f6c1d6ccbe1d9e40b179b00883ec [ diff ] 
Content: Migrate AudioFocusDelegate to JniRawPtr

AudioFocusDelegate stores its native
::content::AudioFocusDelegateAndroid pointer in a raw Java long field.
Passing untyped integer handles across the JNI boundary bypasses
compile-time type checking and lifetime tracking, risking use-after-free
bugs when the native object is destroyed.

Declare a package-private NativeAudioFocusDelegateAndroid JniTypeToken
mapped to ::content::AudioFocusDelegateAndroid and store the borrowed
reference in a JniRawPtr<NativeAudioFocusDelegateAndroid> created via
jni_zero::MakeRaw. Pass the reference to @NativeMethods calls via
JniPtr<NativeAudioFocusDelegateAndroid> and release the handle on native
teardown.

[AIM]: Key the compose button's hover highlight to the button

Before:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NTcyMzM3NDQ0ODgwMzg0MHxkNzllODhlMC00OQ

After:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NDU0NzAzNjE5MDg2NzQ1NnwzZTMzMTZiNi1jMg

The highlight was keyed to :hover on the cr-searchbox-compose-button
host, whose box extends past the inner button's trailing edge (5px in
the full WebUI omnibox; Omnibox Everywhere has the same rule) through
the glow container's padding and margin, so hovering that strip lit the
chip with nothing to click. The button's own :hover now sets a custom
property that the highlight's opacity reads, keeping the fade. The glow
ring is unchanged.

[Updater] Delete corrupted or non-dict prefs.json

Previously, when prefs.json contained invalid JSON (and moving it to
prefs.bad failed, such as when prefs.bad was a non-empty directory) or
valid non-dictionary JSON, CreatePrefService retried reading the same
broken file for 2 minutes before failing with 0x80040805. Additionally,
the AppServer methods ModeCheck and SwapVersions dereferenced the
returned value of CreateLocalPrefs without checking for nullptr.

Delete prefs.json when initialization fails with
PREF_READ_ERROR_JSON_PARSE, PREF_READ_ERROR_JSON_TYPE, or
PREF_READ_ERROR_JSON_REPEAT so the next retry cleanly recreates it, and
guard against null LocalPrefs in AppServer.

Fixed: 573454031
Test: updater_tests --gtest_filter="PrefsTest.*:PrefsInvalidFileTest.*:AppServerTestCase.*"

parent
e9c2d318f7189b3be861ae3709a0ac53c4df9395 [ diff ] 
[DW] Keep caption bar transparent until uncustomized bar is present

On some Android 16 tablet devices, the OS ignores task appearance
updates during the drag-to-desktop transition animation and inflates
the caption view asynchronously, leaving an opaque caption bar over
the tab strip until focus changes.

- Evaluate caption bar transparency on every caption bar insets update
  rather than only when desktop windowing mode changes.
- Keep the caption bar background transparent while Chrome customizes
  the app header or when no caption bar bounding rects are present
  (e.g. in fullscreen, or while transitioning into desktop windowing
  mode).
- Clear the transparent appearance only when a caption bar with
  bounding rects is present and Chrome does not customize it.

Skip rule set invalidation for an unstyled, dirty root
📋 Metadata
commit
1c2f8ce14c8b3bb3549a007e545cfd153bb10b21
[ log ]
author
Zhou Shuangshuang <shuangshuang.zhou@intel.com>
Tue Nov 03 00:54:04 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 03 00:54:04 2026
tree
0ae7159a61ad9ffb400dbe84ec092bd08fbdc293
parent
b07b0feafaa7151c86687c24488bc0d3691ad7e2 [ diff ] 
Skip rule set invalidation for an unstyled, dirty root

When stylesheets are added to a TreeScope, InvalidateForRuleSetChanges()
sets up a SelectorFilter for all ancestors and matches the changed rules
against the invalidation root (the shadow host, or the document
element). The only possible outcome for the root is a local style
recalc. If the root has no ComputedStyle yet,
ApplyRuleSetInvalidationForTreeScope() also does not descend into the
tree below it.

So when the root is unstyled and already marked for style recalc, the
whole pass has no effect. This is the common case for a shadow root that
is created and given stylesheets right after its host is inserted, as
custom elements usually do. Return early in that case.

6f15979c63c06bba23ae4fbababb94dc057abe7c
[ log ]

tree
291cc80a92ffcf136ae137377576baab5b697d25
parent
cc3010a7956437fbeb36acc942c73f8a06cf24a2 [ diff ] 
[Fixit] Add regression test for closing crashed tab needing reload

When a crashed tab showing the SadTab overlay is closed,
TabModel::DestroyTabFeatures() calls tab_features_.reset(), setting
tab->GetTabFeatures() to nullptr before running ~TabFeatures(). During
~TabFeatures(), ~SadTabHelper() detaches the crashed overlay view and
restores visibility to the underlying WebView. If the crashed tab's
NavigationController has NeedsReload() set, becoming visible
synchronously triggers LoadIfNecessary() and notifies TabStripModel
observers (calling TabUIHelper::GetFavicon()) while TabFeatures is
mid-destruction.

This crash was resolved by https://crrev.com/c/8544755, which migrated
deferred tab state into TabUIHelper and removed the unguarded
tab().GetTabFeatures() dereference in TabUIHelper::GetFavicon(). Add a
regression test covering this teardown path.

7e328b4af0979f593968f7e1fe5c5a12930b3ec0 [ diff ] 
[ipc] Re-enable `IPCMessageIntegrity.ReadVectorTooLarge1` on Android

`IPCMessageIntegrity.ReadVectorTooLarge1` (formerly in
`ipc/ipc_fuzzing_tests.cc`, merged into `ipc/ipc_message_unittest.cc` in
https://crrev.com/c/6931446) was disabled on Android in
https://crrev.com/1259823002 when migrating `ipc_tests` off the legacy
`build/android/pylib/gtest/filter/ipc_tests_disabled` filter file.

The test is a single-process unit test that writes an element count of
`0x21000003` followed by two `int64_t` values into an `IPC::Message` and
verifies that `ReadParam()` into a `std::vector<int64_t>` returns
`false`. In `ParamTraits<std::vector<P>>::Read`
(`ipc/param_traits_utils.h`), the bounds check `size > INT_MAX /
sizeof(P)` (updated to use `size_t` via
`base::PickleIterator::ReadLength()` in https://crrev.com/c/3727447)
immediately rejects `0x21000003` (`> INT_MAX / sizeof(int64_t)`) and
returns `false` before `r->resize(size)` is called, matching the
adjacent `ReadVectorTooLarge2` test that is already enabled on Android.

Re-enable `IPCMessageIntegrity.ReadVectorTooLarge1` on Android, and add
documentation comments to all tests in `ipc/ipc_message_unittest.cc`.
Verified on Android 15 phone and tablet emulators.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

Optimize Sea Pen template lookup tables using fixed flat maps and spans

Replace std::map and std::set in sea_pen_utils_generated with compile-time base::fixed_flat_map and base::span over consteval-sorted arrays. This avoids runtime heap allocations and removes the need for base::NoDestructor.


Update sea_pen_metadata_utils.cc to query the spans using std::ranges::contains and std::ranges::binary_search.

[infobars] Add promotional bit to InfoBarSpec and enforce PromotionsEnabled

Add InfoBarSpec::is_promotional() and Builder::SetIsPromotional(bool) so
that promotional infobars can be declaratively marked at registration
time.

Update BrowserInfoBarManager::Show(), Replace(), and ShowGlobally() to
centrally suppress promotional InfoBarSpecs when the PromotionsEnabled
enterprise policy (prefs::kPromotionsEnabled in local_state) is disabled.

Browser, Session Restore, Startup Launch) as well as PDF and Installer
Downloader infobar specs with SetIsPromotional(true).

[iOS][Actor] Remove ActorService::RequestTabObservation

`GeminiActuationHandler` no longer requests tab observations:
`ActorService` takes them after acts and confirmed interrupts. Remove
the unused public command and its posted-reply plumbing so that tab
observation stays internal to the actor model.

- Document the post-act observation on `PerformActions`.
- Point the `pending_observations_` TODO at the observation bug.
- Move the WebState-went-away error TODO to the extraction completion.

db8fd0795b5b9eae11f91b24103270b67dfb9b96
parent
f6d84a55cffa8af2d5f01ba5ae13380b721c27f4 [ diff ] 
[UI] Re-enable `AcceleratorTest.GetShortcutText` on Android

`AcceleratorTest.GetShortcutText` was disabled on Android in
https://crrev.com/c/1682516 because `Accelerator::GetShortcutText()`
crashed on Android builders when looking up localized modifier and key
names (`IDS_APP_CTRL_KEY`, `IDS_APP_SHIFT_KEY`, `IDS_APP_ALT_KEY`, and
`IDS_APP_COMMA_KEY`) via `l10n_util::GetStringUTF16()`. That failure was
triggered after https://crrev.com/c/1682047 moved `UNCOMPRESSED_LOCALES`
out of `BuildConfig` into `sUncompressedLocales` in
`org.chromium.ui.base.ResourceBundle`, causing
`getLocalePakResourcePath()` to assert when `sUncompressedLocales` was
unset in unit tests or when `en-US.pak` was not extracted with a version
suffix by `ResourceExtractor`.

The underlying Android locale `.pak` loading issues in unit tests were
resolved by https://crrev.com/c/1918367, which updated
`ResourceBundle.getLocalePakResourcePath()` to return `null` instead of
asserting when `sUncompressedLocales` is `null` in unit tests so
`ResourceBundle::LoadLocaleResources()` falls back to the side-loaded
`en-US.pak` on disk, and https://crrev.com/c/2684956, which removed
`ResourceExtractor` and simplified Android locale `.pak` loading in
`ui/base/resource/resource_bundle_android.cc`. Additionally,
https://crrev.com/c/6442193 simplified `Accelerator::GetShortcutText()`
to join `GetShortcutVectorRepresentation()` with `"+"`.

Re-enable `AcceleratorTest.GetShortcutText` on Android.

Using Buganizer issue 41468741 corresponding to old Chromium bug tracker
issue 980267.

Verified on Android 15 phone and Android 15 tablet emulators.

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

parent
0b368fa056d13b8a442e778e78e18a9ac461bf98 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Delegate Voice Plate intent creation to VoicePlateIntegration

Move the Google app specific parts of VoicePlateIntentFactory behind
VoicePlateIntegration: the VoicePlateActivity component, the
resolvability and signature checks, and the extras (account hint, color
scheme, Stop button). They now live in the //clank
VoicePlateIntegrationImpl.

Add VoicePlateIntegration#isEligible(Profile) and remove
VoicePlateIntentFactory. In Chromium builds the integration reports
requests as ineligible and never supplies an intent, so voice
recognition falls back to the system speech recognizer.

This also drops the //chrome/browser/gsa dependency from the omnibox
targets.
